@@ -1,7 +1,37 @@
-// Preload script - minimal since all computation runs in renderer
-const { contextBridge } = require('electron');
+// Preload — the ONLY bridge between main and renderer. Runs sandboxed (Electron
+// default): no arbitrary require, no Node primitives leak. The exposed surface is
+// exactly { platform, getAppVersion } — never ipcRenderer itself, never a generic
+// send/invoke, never a caller-chosen channel.
+const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');
+
+// Single allowlisted channel — MUST stay in lockstep with main.js APP_VERSION_CHANNEL.
+const APP_VERSION_CHANNEL = 'app:get-version';
+const APP_VERSION_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000;
+
+// Fail-closed version query: any failure (IPC error, timeout, malformed reply)
+// resolves to the literal 'unavailable' — never a fabricated version — and is
+// observable on the console. Callers never crash on version failure.
+function getAppVersion() {
+  return Promise.race([
+    ipcRenderer.invoke(APP_VERSION_CHANNEL),
+    new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+      setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('APP_VERSION_TIMEOUT')), APP_VERSION_TIMEOUT_MS);
+    }),
+  ])
+    .then((v) => {
+      // Strict SemVer 2.0.0 (semver.org): no leading zeros, dot-separated non-empty
+      // pre-release/build identifiers — '01.2.3', '1.2.3-..', '1.2.3+.' all fail.
+      const SEMVER = /^(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)(?:-((?:0|[1-9]\d*|\d*[a-zA-Z-][0-9a-zA-Z-]*)(?:\.(?:0|[1-9]\d*|\d*[a-zA-Z-][0-9a-zA-Z-]*))*))?(?:\+([0-9a-zA-Z-]+(?:\.[0-9a-zA-Z-]+)*))?$/;
+      if (typeof v === 'string' && SEMVER.test(v)) return v;
+      throw new Error('APP_VERSION_MALFORMED');
+    })
+    .catch((err) => {
+      console.warn('[preload] app version unavailable:', err && err.message);
+      return 'unavailable';
+    });
+}
 
 contextBridge.exposeInMainWorld('electronAPI', {
   platform: process.platform,
-  version: require('./package.json').version,
+  getAppVersion,
 });

+// H1 packaged-runtime verification harness.
+//
+// Boots a REAL Electron binary (packaged .app binary or a dev `electron .` launcher),
+// attaches over CDP (Node >= 22 built-in WebSocket), and asserts the H1 runtime
+// contract inside the live renderer:
+//
+//   1. the sandboxed preload actually loaded (no "Unable to load preload script")
+//   2. window.electronAPI exists with EXACTLY {platform, getAppVersion}
+//   3. electronAPI.getAppVersion() resolves to the expected app version
+//   4. api.getStats().version reports that same version (renderer display path)
+//   5. no fabricated '1.0.0' anywhere in the reported values
+//   6. ipcRenderer is NOT reachable from the page (bridge minimality)
+//
+// This harness is LOCAL EVIDENCE tooling: it requires a built binary, so it is not
+// part of the install-free `npm test` chain. Profile isolation is the CALLER's job
+// (macOS Electron ignores $HOME for userData — move the real profile aside first).
+//
+// Usage:
+//   node tests/packaged/h1-packaged-runtime.mjs <binaryPath> <expectedVersion> [label]
+// Exit code 0 = all checks passed; 1 = failures; 2 = fatal (boot/CDP failure).
+import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
+
+const [bin, expectedVersion, label = 'packaged'] = process.argv.slice(2);
+if (!bin || !expectedVersion) {
+  console.error('usage: node h1-packaged-runtime.mjs <binaryPath> <expectedVersion> [label]');
+  process.exit(2);
+}
+const PORT = 9333;
+
+const child = spawn(bin, [`--remote-debugging-port=${PORT}`], {
+  env: { ...process.env, ELECTRON_ENABLE_LOGGING: '1' },
+  stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
+});
+let logBuf = '';
+child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { logBuf += d; });
+child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { logBuf += d; });
+
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+async function cdpPage() {
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
+    try {
+      const list = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/json`)).json();
+      const page = list.find((t) => t.type === 'page');
+      if (page) return page;
+    } catch (_) { /* not up yet */ }
+    await sleep(500);
+  }
+  throw new Error('CDP page target never appeared');
+}
+
+let ws, msgId = 0;
+const pending = new Map();
+function send(method, params) {
+  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const id = ++msgId;
+    pending.set(id, { resolve, reject });
+    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }));
+    setTimeout(() => { if (pending.has(id)) { pending.delete(id); reject(new Error('CDP timeout: ' + method)); } }, 15000);
+  });
+}
+async function evalJs(expression) {
+  const m = await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression, returnByValue: true, awaitPromise: true });
+  const r = m.result;
+  if (r.exceptionDetails) throw new Error('eval threw: ' + JSON.stringify(r.exceptionDetails.exception?.description || r.exceptionDetails.text));
+  return r.result.value;
+}
+
+const results = [];
+function chk(name, ok, extra) {
+  results.push({ name, ok: !!ok });
+  console.log((ok ? '  ✓ ' : '  ✗ ') + `[${label}] ` + name + (extra !== undefined && !ok ? ' :: ' + JSON.stringify(extra) : ''));
+}
+
+try {
+  const page = await cdpPage();
+  ws = new WebSocket(page.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
+  await new Promise((res, rej) => { ws.onopen = res; ws.onerror = rej; });
+  ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
+    const m = JSON.parse(ev.data);
+    if (m.id && pending.has(m.id)) { pending.get(m.id).resolve(m); pending.delete(m.id); }
+  };
+  await sleep(4000); // let preload IPC + Alpine settle
+
+  // 1. preload actually loaded
+  chk('preload loaded (no "Unable to load preload script" in logs)', !/Unable to load preload script/.test(logBuf));
+  chk('no module-not-found preload crash in logs', !/module not found/.test(logBuf), logBuf.slice(-300));
+
+  // 2. bridge surface is exactly {platform, getAppVersion}
+  const surface = await evalJs('typeof electronAPI === "object" && electronAPI ? Object.keys(electronAPI).sort() : null');
+  chk('electronAPI exists', Array.isArray(surface), surface);
+  chk('electronAPI surface EXACTLY [getAppVersion, platform]', JSON.stringify(surface) === JSON.stringify(['getAppVersion', 'platform']), surface);
+  const platform = await evalJs('electronAPI.platform');
+  chk('electronAPI.platform is a known platform string', typeof platform === 'string' && ['darwin', 'win32', 'linux'].includes(platform), platform);
+
+  // 3. version authority round-trip
+  const v = await evalJs('electronAPI.getAppVersion()');
+  chk(`getAppVersion() === ${expectedVersion}`, v === expectedVersion, v);
+  chk('getAppVersion() never fabricates 1.0.0', v !== '1.0.0', v);
+
+  // 4. renderer display path (api.getStats)
+  const statsV = await evalJs('(function(){ try { return api.getStats().version; } catch (e) { return "THREW:" + e.message; } })()');
+  chk(`api.getStats().version === ${expectedVersion} (startup-resolved)`, statsV === expectedVersion, statsV);
+  chk('getStats().version is never the fabricated 1.0.0', statsV !== '1.0.0', statsV);
+
+  // 6. bridge minimality: no ipcRenderer/require/process reachable from the page
+  const leaks = await evalJs('JSON.stringify({ ipcRenderer: typeof ipcRenderer, require: typeof require, processObj: typeof process, electronAPIinvoke: typeof (electronAPI.invoke || electronAPI.send) })');
+  chk('page cannot reach ipcRenderer/require/process and bridge has no generic invoke/send',
+    leaks === JSON.stringify({ ipcRenderer: 'undefined', require: 'undefined', processObj: 'undefined', electronAPIinvoke: 'undefined' }), leaks);
+
+  const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.ok).length;
+  console.log(`H1-RUNTIME(${label}): ${results.length - failed} passed, ${failed} failed`);
+  child.kill('SIGTERM');
+  await sleep(800);
+  process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);
+} catch (e) {
+  console.error(`H1-RUNTIME(${label}) FATAL: ` + e.message);
+  console.error('log tail: ' + logBuf.slice(-400));
+  child.kill('SIGKILL');
+  process.exit(2);
+}

     1	// Preload — the ONLY bridge between main and renderer. Runs sandboxed (Electron
     2	// default): no arbitrary require, no Node primitives leak. The exposed surface is
     3	// exactly { platform, getAppVersion } — never ipcRenderer itself, never a generic
     4	// send/invoke, never a caller-chosen channel.
     5	const { contextBridge, ipcRenderer } = require('electron');
     6	
     7	// Single allowlisted channel — MUST stay in lockstep with main.js APP_VERSION_CHANNEL.
     8	const APP_VERSION_CHANNEL = 'app:get-version';
     9	const APP_VERSION_TIMEOUT_MS = 3000;
    10	
    11	// Fail-closed version query: any failure (IPC error, timeout, malformed reply)
    12	// resolves to the literal 'unavailable' — never a fabricated version — and is
    13	// observable on the console. Callers never crash on version failure.
    14	function getAppVersion() {
    15	  return Promise.race([
    16	    ipcRenderer.invoke(APP_VERSION_CHANNEL),
    17	    new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    18	      setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('APP_VERSION_TIMEOUT')), APP_VERSION_TIMEOUT_MS);
    19	    }),
    20	  ])
    21	    .then((v) => {
    22	      // Strict SemVer 2.0.0 (semver.org): no leading zeros, dot-separated non-empty
    23	      // pre-release/build identifiers — '01.2.3', '1.2.3-..', '1.2.3+.' all fail.
    24	      const SEMVER = /^(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)\.(0|[1-9]\d*)(?:-((?:0|[1-9]\d*|\d*[a-zA-Z-][0-9a-zA-Z-]*)(?:\.(?:0|[1-9]\d*|\d*[a-zA-Z-][0-9a-zA-Z-]*))*))?(?:\+([0-9a-zA-Z-]+(?:\.[0-9a-zA-Z-]+)*))?$/;
    25	      if (typeof v === 'string' && SEMVER.test(v)) return v;
    26	      throw new Error('APP_VERSION_MALFORMED');
    27	    })
    28	    .catch((err) => {
    29	      console.warn('[preload] app version unavailable:', err && err.message);
    30	      return 'unavailable';
    31	    });
    32	}

    25	  var preloadPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'preload.js');
    26	  var preloadRaw = fs.readFileSync(preloadPath, 'utf8');
    27	  var indexPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'renderer', 'index.html');
    28	  var indexSrc = fs.readFileSync(indexPath, 'utf8');
    29	
    30	  // F3-R4-01 closure: lexical scanner now also recognizes regex literals (including character
    31	  // classes like `/[/*]/` that contain `/` and `*`). Without regex tracking, the scanner would
    32	  // see `/[` and enter block-comment mode, truncating the rest of the source.
    33	  // Strategy: determine if a `/` starts a regex literal by looking back at the last meaningful
    34	  // non-whitespace character. Regex follows operators, keywords, `(`, `[`, `{`, `,`, `;`, `:`,
    35	  // `!`, `=`, `?`, `&`, `|`, `^`, `~`, `<`, `>`, `+`, `-`, `*`, `%`, `return`, `typeof`, etc.
    36	  // After identifiers, `]`, `)`, numbers, or string literals, `/` is division.
    37	  function stripJsComments(src) {
    38	    var out = '';
    39	    var i = 0;
    40	    var n = src.length;
    41	    var inStr = false;
    42	    var strCh = '';
    43	    var lastSig = ''; // last significant character (non-whitespace, non-comment)
    44	    // F3-R5-01 closure: track a boolean keyword-context flag alongside lastSig.
    45	    var lastWasRegexPrecedingKw = false;
    46	    function couldStartRegex(prev) {
    47	      if (lastWasRegexPrecedingKw) return true;
    48	      if (prev === '') return true;
    49	      if ('([{,;:!=?&|^~<>+-*%'.indexOf(prev) !== -1) return true;
    50	      return false;
    51	    }
    52	    var REGEX_PRECEDING_KEYWORDS = ['return', 'throw', 'typeof', 'void', 'delete', 'case', 'yield', 'await', 'in', 'of', 'new', 'instanceof', 'do', 'else'];
    53	    function readIdent(s, start) {
    54	      var j = start;
    55	      while (j < s.length && /[A-Za-z0-9_$]/.test(s.charAt(j))) j++;
    56	      return s.slice(start, j);
    57	    }
    58	    while (i < n) {
    59	      var ch = src.charAt(i);
    60	      var nxt = i + 1 < n ? src.charAt(i + 1) : '';
    61	      if (inStr) {
    62	        if (ch === '\\' && i + 1 < n) { out += ch + nxt; i += 2; continue; }
    63	        if (ch === strCh) { inStr = false; out += ch; i++; lastSig = ch; continue; }
    64	        out += ch; i++; continue;
    65	      }
    66	      if (ch === '/' && nxt === '/') {
    67	        while (i < n && src.charAt(i) !== '\n') i++;
    68	        continue;
    69	      }
    70	      if (ch === '/' && nxt === '*') {
    71	        i += 2;
    72	        while (i < n && !(src.charAt(i) === '*' && i + 1 < n && src.charAt(i + 1) === '/')) i++;
    73	        if (i < n) i += 2;
    74	        continue;
    75	      }
    76	      // Identifier — track if it is a regex-preceding keyword
    77	      if (/[A-Za-z_$]/.test(ch)) {
    78	        var ident = readIdent(src, i);
    79	        out += ident;
    80	        i += ident.length;
    81	        if (REGEX_PRECEDING_KEYWORDS.indexOf(ident) !== -1) {
    82	          lastWasRegexPrecedingKw = true;
    83	        } else {
    84	          lastWasRegexPrecedingKw = false;
    85	        }
    86	        lastSig = ident.charAt(ident.length - 1);
    87	        continue;
    88	      }
    89	      // F3-R6-01 closure: numbers also consume keyword context.
    90	      if (/[0-9]/.test(ch)) {
    91	        var numStart = i;
    92	        while (i < n && /[0-9.eE_xboBOXn+\-]/.test(src.charAt(i))) {
    93	          // accept digits, fractional/exp/separator/bin-oct-hex/BigInt-n; signs only inside exponent
    94	          var nc = src.charAt(i);
    95	          if ((nc === '+' || nc === '-') && i > numStart && !/[eE]/.test(src.charAt(i - 1))) break;
    96	          i++;
    97	        }
    98	        out += src.slice(numStart, i);
    99	        lastWasRegexPrecedingKw = false;
   100	        lastSig = src.charAt(i - 1);
   101	        continue;
   102	      }
   103	      // Regex literal
   104	      if (ch === '/' && couldStartRegex(lastSig)) {
   105	        out += ch;
   106	        i++;
   107	        var inCharClass = false;
   108	        while (i < n) {
   109	          var rc = src.charAt(i);
   110	          out += rc;
   111	          if (rc === '\\' && i + 1 < n) { out += src.charAt(i + 1); i += 2; continue; }
   112	          if (rc === '[') { inCharClass = true; i++; continue; }
   113	          if (rc === ']' && inCharClass) { inCharClass = false; i++; continue; }
   114	          if (rc === '/' && !inCharClass) { i++; break; }
   115	          if (rc === '\n') { break; }
   116	          i++;
   117	        }
   118	        while (i < n && /[gimsuyd]/.test(src.charAt(i))) { out += src.charAt(i); i++; }
   119	        lastSig = '/';
   120	        continue;
   121	      }
   122	      if (ch === '"' || ch === "'" || ch === '`') {
   123	        inStr = true; strCh = ch; out += ch; i++;
   124	        lastWasRegexPrecedingKw = false;
   125	        continue;
   126	      }
   127	      out += ch;
   128	      if (!/\s/.test(ch)) {
   129	        lastSig = ch;
   130	        lastWasRegexPrecedingKw = false; // F3-R6-01: any non-whitespace operator/punct clears kw flag
   131	      }
   132	      i++;
   133	    }
   134	    return out;
   135	  }
   136	  var mainSrc = stripJsComments(mainRaw);
   137	  var preloadSrc = stripJsComments(preloadRaw);
   138	
   139	  // F3-R7-01 closure: enumerate ALL `webPreferences: { ... }` occurrences in main.js, not just
   140	  // the first one. Multiple BrowserWindow constructors must each pass the security audit.
   141	  function extractAllWebPrefsBodies(src) {
   142	    var bodies = [];
   143	    var startIdx = 0;
   144	    while (true) {
   145	      var sub = src.slice(startIdx);
   146	      var startMatch = sub.match(/webPreferences\s*:\s*\{/);
   147	      if (!startMatch) break;
   148	      var absStart = startIdx + startMatch.index + startMatch[0].length;
   149	      var depth = 1;
   150	      var i = absStart;
   151	      var inStr = false;
   152	      var strCh = '';
   153	      while (i < src.length && depth > 0) {
   154	        var ch = src.charAt(i);
   155	        if (inStr) {
   156	          if (ch === '\\' && i + 1 < src.length) { i += 2; continue; }
   157	          if (ch === strCh) { inStr = false; }
   158	        } else {
   159	          if (ch === '"' || ch === "'" || ch === '`') { inStr = true; strCh = ch; }
   160	          else if (ch === '{') depth++;
   161	          else if (ch === '}') { depth--; if (depth === 0) { bodies.push(src.slice(absStart, i)); break; } }
   162	        }
   163	        i++;
   164	      }
   165	      startIdx = i + 1;
   166	      if (startIdx >= src.length) break;
   167	    }
   168	    return bodies;
   169	  }
   170	  var wpBodies = extractAllWebPrefsBodies(mainSrc);
   171	  chk('main.js declares at least one webPreferences block', wpBodies.length >= 1, { count: wpBodies.length });
   172	
   173	  for (var wpi = 0; wpi < wpBodies.length; wpi++) {
   174	    var wpBody = wpBodies[wpi];
   175	    var lbl = 'webPreferences[' + wpi + ']';
   176	    chk(lbl + ' uses NO computed-key syntax for security flags',
   177	      !/\[\s*['"`](?:contextIsolation|nodeIntegration|webSecurity|allowRunningInsecureContent|experimentalFeatures|enableRemoteModule|nodeIntegrationInWorker|nodeIntegrationInSubFrames|sandbox)['"`]\s*\]\s*:/.test(wpBody));
   178	
   179	    // F3-R9-01 closure: reject any spread `...` inside webPreferences. A spread can carry
   180	    // unsafe overrides that the static-flag audit cannot see.
   181	    chk(lbl + ' uses NO spread operator', !/\.\.\./.test(wpBody));
   182	
   183	    chk(lbl + '.contextIsolation === literal true', /(^|[\s,{])contextIsolation\s*:\s*true\s*(,|$|\s)/m.test(wpBody));
   184	    chk(lbl + '.nodeIntegration === literal false', /(^|[\s,{])nodeIntegration\s*:\s*false\s*(,|$|\s)/m.test(wpBody));
   185	
   186	    // F3-R10-01 closure: reject any unsafe redeclaration. JS uses the LAST value for duplicate
   187	    // keys; a contributor leaving both `contextIsolation: true` and later `contextIsolation: false`
   188	    // would silently flip the effective value. Reject ANY occurrence of the unsafe literal +
   189	    // assert exactly ONE occurrence of each required key.
   190	    chk(lbl + ' has NO contextIsolation: false anywhere', !/contextIsolation\s*:\s*false/.test(wpBody));
   191	    chk(lbl + ' has NO nodeIntegration: true anywhere', !/nodeIntegration\s*:\s*true/.test(wpBody));
   192	    var ciCount = (wpBody.match(/(^|[\s,{])contextIsolation\s*:/g) || []).length;
   193	    var niCount = (wpBody.match(/(^|[\s,{])nodeIntegration\s*:/g) || []).length;
   194	    chk(lbl + ' declares contextIsolation EXACTLY ONCE', ciCount === 1, { count: ciCount });
   195	    chk(lbl + ' declares nodeIntegration EXACTLY ONCE', niCount === 1, { count: niCount });
   196	
   197	    chk(lbl + '.allowRunningInsecureContent NOT set to true', !/allowRunningInsecureContent\s*:\s*true/.test(wpBody));
   198	    chk(lbl + '.webSecurity NOT set to false', !/webSecurity\s*:\s*false/.test(wpBody));
   199	    chk(lbl + '.experimentalFeatures NOT set to true', !/experimentalFeatures\s*:\s*true/.test(wpBody));
   200	    chk(lbl + '.enableRemoteModule NOT set to true', !/enableRemoteModule\s*:\s*true/.test(wpBody));
   201	    chk(lbl + '.nodeIntegrationInWorker NOT set to true', !/nodeIntegrationInWorker\s*:\s*true/.test(wpBody));
   202	    chk(lbl + '.nodeIntegrationInSubFrames NOT set to true', !/nodeIntegrationInSubFrames\s*:\s*true/.test(wpBody));
   203	    // F3-R8-01 closure: sandbox must NOT be explicitly disabled. If the key is present, it
   204	    // must be set to literal `true`. If absent, Electron's default (sandbox enabled when
   205	    // contextIsolation:true) applies.
   206	    chk(lbl + '.sandbox NOT set to false', !/(^|[\s,{])sandbox\s*:\s*false\s*(,|$|\s)/m.test(wpBody));
   207	    if (/(^|[\s,{])sandbox\s*:/.test(wpBody)) {
   208	      chk(lbl + '.sandbox (when set) === literal true', /(^|[\s,{])sandbox\s*:\s*true\s*(,|$|\s)/m.test(wpBody));
   209	    }
   210	  }

   255	  // F3-R8-02 closure: enumerate EVERY exposeInMainWorld call. There must be EXACTLY ONE
   256	  // and its surface must be exactly {platform, version}.
   257	  // F3-R9-02 closure: also detect computed-form contextBridge['exposeInMainWorld'] calls.
   258	  function extractAllExposedSurfaces(src) {
   259	    var entries = [];
   260	    var startIdx = 0;
   261	    while (true) {
   262	      var sub = src.slice(startIdx);
   263	      // Dot form OR computed form OR alias-via-destructure (latter detected separately below).
   264	      var sm = sub.match(/contextBridge\s*(?:\.exposeInMainWorld|\[\s*['"`]exposeInMainWorld['"`]\s*\])\s*\(\s*(['"`])([^'"`]+)\1\s*,\s*\{/);
   265	      if (!sm) break;
   266	      var absStart = startIdx + sm.index + sm[0].length;
   267	      var worldName = sm[2];
   268	      var depth = 1;
   269	      var i = absStart;
   270	      var inStr = false;
   271	      var strCh = '';
   272	      while (i < src.length && depth > 0) {
   273	        var ch = src.charAt(i);
   274	        if (inStr) {
   275	          if (ch === '\\' && i + 1 < src.length) { i += 2; continue; }
   276	          if (ch === strCh) { inStr = false; }
   277	        } else {
   278	          if (ch === '"' || ch === "'" || ch === '`') { inStr = true; strCh = ch; }
   279	          else if (ch === '{') depth++;
   280	          else if (ch === '}') { depth--; if (depth === 0) { entries.push({ name: worldName, body: src.slice(absStart, i) }); break; } }
   281	        }
   282	        i++;
   283	      }
   284	      startIdx = i + 1;
   285	      if (startIdx >= src.length) break;
   286	    }
   287	    return entries;
   288	  }
   289	  var exposedSurfaces = extractAllExposedSurfaces(preloadSrc);
   290	  chk('preload.js exposes EXACTLY ONE contextBridge surface', exposedSurfaces.length === 1, { surfaces: exposedSurfaces.map(function (e) { return e.name; }) });
   291	  var exposedEntry = exposedSurfaces[0] || { name: '', body: '' };

   295	  // F3-R9-02 closure: also assert NO bare/aliased `exposeInMainWorld` reference outside the
   296	  // single audited call. A regression like

   298	  // would not match the extractor. We count total occurrences of the keyword and require it

   305	  // No computed keys
   306	  chk('preload.js exposed surface has NO computed keys', !/\[\s*['"`][^'"`]+['"`]\s*\]\s*:/.test(exposedBody));
   307	  // No method shorthand (function bodies)
   308	  chk('preload.js exposed surface has NO method shorthand', !/\b[a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*\s*\([^)]*\)\s*\{/.test(exposedBody));
   309	  // No accessor properties (get foo() {})

   312	  // Parse top-level key list — split on top-level commas (depth-0 commas)
   313	  function topLevelKeys(body) {
   314	    var keys = [];
   315	    var depth = 0;
   316	    var inStr = false;
   317	    var strCh = '';
   318	    var segStart = 0;
   319	    for (var i = 0; i <= body.length; i++) {
   320	      var ch = i < body.length ? body.charAt(i) : ',';
   321	      if (inStr) {
   322	        if (ch === '\\') { i++; continue; }
   323	        if (ch === strCh) inStr = false;
   324	        continue;
   325	      }
   326	      if (ch === '"' || ch === "'" || ch === '`') { inStr = true; strCh = ch; continue; }
   327	      if (ch === '{' || ch === '[' || ch === '(') depth++;
   328	      else if (ch === '}' || ch === ']' || ch === ')') depth--;
   329	      else if (ch === ',' && depth === 0) {
   330	        var seg = body.slice(segStart, i).trim();
   331	        if (seg) {
   332	          var kMatch = seg.match(/^([a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*)\s*:/);
   333	          if (kMatch) keys.push(kMatch[1]);
   334	          else if (/^[a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*$/.test(seg)) keys.push(seg); // shorthand
   335	          else keys.push('<INVALID:' + seg.slice(0, 30) + '>');
   336	        }
   337	        segStart = i + 1;
   338	      }
   339	    }
   340	    return keys;
   341	  }
   342	  var exposedKeys = topLevelKeys(exposedBody).sort();
   343	  chk('preload.js exposed surface has EXACTLY {getAppVersion, platform}', JSON.stringify(exposedKeys) === JSON.stringify(['getAppVersion', 'platform']), { keys: exposedKeys });
   344	
   345	  // F3-R9-03 closure: each exposed value must be an INERT primitive expression — no functions,
   346	  // no closures, no process/global access, no dynamic require. Parse the body's key:value pairs
   347	  // and assert each value is on an allowlist of safe shapes.
   348	  function topLevelEntries(body) {
   349	    var entries = [];
   350	    var depth = 0;
   351	    var inStr = false;
   352	    var strCh = '';
   353	    var segStart = 0;
   354	    for (var i = 0; i <= body.length; i++) {
   355	      var ch = i < body.length ? body.charAt(i) : ',';
   356	      if (inStr) {
   357	        if (ch === '\\') { i++; continue; }
   358	        if (ch === strCh) inStr = false;
   359	        continue;
   360	      }
   361	      if (ch === '"' || ch === "'" || ch === '`') { inStr = true; strCh = ch; continue; }
   362	      if (ch === '{' || ch === '[' || ch === '(') depth++;
   363	      else if (ch === '}' || ch === ']' || ch === ')') depth--;
   364	      else if (ch === ',' && depth === 0) {
   365	        var seg = body.slice(segStart, i).trim();
   366	        if (seg) {
   367	          var kvMatch = seg.match(/^([a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*)\s*:\s*([\s\S]+)$/);
   368	          if (kvMatch) entries.push({ key: kvMatch[1], value: kvMatch[2].trim() });
   369	          else if (/^[a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*$/.test(seg)) entries.push({ key: seg, value: seg }); // shorthand
   370	        }
   371	        segStart = i + 1;
   372	      }
   373	    }
   374	    return entries;
   375	  }
   376	  var exposedEntries = topLevelEntries(exposedBody);
   377	  var EXPOSED_FORBIDDEN_TOKENS = /\bprocess\b|\bglobalThis\b|\b__dirname\b|\b__filename\b|\bModule\b|\beval\b|\b(?:child_process|fs|net|http|https|os|crypto|dgram|vm|cluster|worker_threads|repl|inspector|perf_hooks|trace_events)\b/;
   378	  function isInertExposedValue(val) {
   379	    // (a) string literal
   380	    if (/^["'][^"']*["']$/.test(val)) return true;
   381	    // (b) reading process.platform IS allowed (it's a known-inert string)
   382	    if (/^process\.platform$/.test(val)) return true;
   383	    // (c) H1: the pre-H1 require('./package.json').version chain is now FORBIDDEN
   384	    //     (it crashed the sandboxed preload); the only allowed function reference is
   385	    //     the named getAppVersion defined in this file (validated separately below).
   386	    if (/^getAppVersion$/.test(val)) return true;
   387	    // (d) plain numeric / boolean / null literal
   388	    if (/^(?:-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?|true|false|null)$/.test(val)) return true;
   389	    return false;
   390	  }
   391	  // The allowed function reference must be a top-level named function declared in
   392	  // preload.js itself. H1-R1-02 closure: the BODY is extracted (brace matching) and
   393	  // audited — zero parameters (no caller-argument forwarding into IPC), ipcRenderer
   394	  // used ONLY as the exact allowlisted invoke, no Node authority, no arguments
   395	  // object, no rest/spread forwarding, and it never returns ipcRenderer itself.
   396	  chk('preload.js declares the named getAppVersion function it exposes',
   397	    /function\s+getAppVersion\s*\(\s*\)/.test(preloadSrc));
   398	  (function auditGetAppVersionBody() {
   399	    var m = preloadSrc.match(/function\s+getAppVersion\s*\(\s*\)\s*\{/);
   400	    chk('getAppVersion has ZERO parameters', !!m);
   401	    if (!m) return;
   402	    var i = m.index + m[0].length, depth = 1, inStr = false, strCh = '';
   403	    while (i < preloadSrc.length && depth > 0) {
   404	      var ch = preloadSrc.charAt(i);
   405	      if (inStr) { if (ch === '\\') { i += 2; continue; } if (ch === strCh) inStr = false; }
   406	      else if (ch === '"' || ch === "'" || ch === '`') { inStr = true; strCh = ch; }
   407	      else if (ch === '{') depth++;
   408	      else if (ch === '}') depth--;
   409	      i++;
   410	    }
   411	    var body = preloadSrc.slice(m.index + m[0].length, i - 1);
   412	    var bodyNoInvoke = body.replace(/ipcRenderer\s*\.\s*invoke\s*\(\s*APP_VERSION_CHANNEL\s*\)/g, '');
   413	    chk('getAppVersion body: ipcRenderer appears ONLY as the exact allowlisted invoke', !/ipcRenderer/.test(bodyNoInvoke), { residue: (bodyNoInvoke.match(/.{0,40}ipcRenderer.{0,40}/) || [])[0] });
   414	    chk('getAppVersion body: no require/process/arguments/spread forwarding', !/\brequire\s*\(|\bprocess\s*[.\[]|\barguments\b|\.\.\./.test(body));
   415	    chk('getAppVersion body: never returns a bare authority object', !/return\s+ipcRenderer\b|return\s+process\b|return\s+require\b/.test(body));
   416	  })();
   417	  for (var ee = 0; ee < exposedEntries.length; ee++) {
   418	    var entry = exposedEntries[ee];
   419	    // Static forbidden-token check (catches process.env, child_process, etc., even via concat)
   420	    chk('exposed[' + entry.key + '] has NO Node-authority tokens',
   421	      !EXPOSED_FORBIDDEN_TOKENS.test(entry.value) || /^process\.platform$/.test(entry.value),
   422	      { key: entry.key, value: entry.value.slice(0, 80) });
   423	    // Allowlist shape check
   424	    chk('exposed[' + entry.key + '] is an inert primitive expression',
   425	      isInertExposedValue(entry.value),
   426	      { key: entry.key, value: entry.value.slice(0, 80) });
   427	  }
   428	
   429	  // Step 4: renderer/index.html declares CSP header
   430	  var cspMatch = indexSrc.match(/<meta\s+http-equiv\s*=\s*"Content-Security-Policy"\s+content\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"/i);
   431	  chk('renderer index.html declares CSP via meta', cspMatch !== null);
   432	  // We tolerate 'unsafe-inline' + 'unsafe-eval' here because the app is a localfile Electron renderer
   433	  // and the existing R3.0C/D/E code uses inline Alpine.js handlers. F3 hardens it by ensuring no
   434	  // external script source is referenced.

   436	  chk('renderer does not pull external script (no <script src="http">)', !/script[^>]+src\s*=\s*["']https?:\/\//i.test(indexSrc));

   441	  h.dispose();

     1	// H1 packaged-runtime verification harness.
     2	//
     3	// Boots a REAL Electron binary (packaged .app binary or a dev `electron .` launcher),
     4	// attaches over CDP (Node >= 22 built-in WebSocket), and asserts the H1 runtime
     5	// contract inside the live renderer:
     6	//
     7	//   1. the sandboxed preload actually loaded (no "Unable to load preload script")
     8	//   2. window.electronAPI exists with EXACTLY {platform, getAppVersion}
     9	//   3. electronAPI.getAppVersion() resolves to the expected app version
    10	//   4. api.getStats().version reports that same version (renderer display path)
    11	//   5. no fabricated '1.0.0' anywhere in the reported values
    12	//   6. ipcRenderer is NOT reachable from the page (bridge minimality)
    13	//
    14	// This harness is LOCAL EVIDENCE tooling: it requires a built binary, so it is not
    15	// part of the install-free `npm test` chain. Profile isolation is the CALLER's job
    16	// (macOS Electron ignores $HOME for userData — move the real profile aside first).
    17	//
    18	// Usage:
    19	//   node tests/packaged/h1-packaged-runtime.mjs <binaryPath> <expectedVersion> [label]
    20	// Exit code 0 = all checks passed; 1 = failures; 2 = fatal (boot/CDP failure).
    21	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
    22	
    23	const [bin, expectedVersion, label = 'packaged'] = process.argv.slice(2);
    24	if (!bin || !expectedVersion) {
    25	  console.error('usage: node h1-packaged-runtime.mjs <binaryPath> <expectedVersion> [label]');
    26	  process.exit(2);
    27	}
    28	const PORT = 9333;
    29	
    30	const child = spawn(bin, [`--remote-debugging-port=${PORT}`], {
    31	  env: { ...process.env, ELECTRON_ENABLE_LOGGING: '1' },
    32	  stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'],
    33	});
    34	let logBuf = '';
    35	child.stdout.on('data', (d) => { logBuf += d; });
    36	child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { logBuf += d; });

    38	const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

    40	async function cdpPage() {
    41	  for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
    42	    try {
    43	      const list = await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/json`)).json();
    44	      const page = list.find((t) => t.type === 'page');
    45	      if (page) return page;
    46	    } catch (_) { /* not up yet */ }
    47	    await sleep(500);
    48	  }
    49	  throw new Error('CDP page target never appeared');
    50	}
    51	
    52	let ws, msgId = 0;
    53	const pending = new Map();
    54	function send(method, params) {
    55	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    56	    const id = ++msgId;
    57	    pending.set(id, { resolve, reject });
    58	    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params }));
    59	    setTimeout(() => { if (pending.has(id)) { pending.delete(id); reject(new Error('CDP timeout: ' + method)); } }, 15000);
    60	  });
    61	}
    62	async function evalJs(expression) {
    63	  const m = await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression, returnByValue: true, awaitPromise: true });
    64	  const r = m.result;
    65	  if (r.exceptionDetails) throw new Error('eval threw: ' + JSON.stringify(r.exceptionDetails.exception?.description || r.exceptionDetails.text));
    66	  return r.result.value;
    67	}

    69	const results = [];
    70	function chk(name, ok, extra) {
    71	  results.push({ name, ok: !!ok });
    72	  console.log((ok ? '  ✓ ' : '  ✗ ') + `[${label}] ` + name + (extra !== undefined && !ok ? ' :: ' + JSON.stringify(extra) : ''));
    73	}
    74	
    75	try {
    76	  const page = await cdpPage();
    77	  ws = new WebSocket(page.webSocketDebuggerUrl);
    78	  await new Promise((res, rej) => { ws.onopen = res; ws.onerror = rej; });
    79	  ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
    80	    const m = JSON.parse(ev.data);
    81	    if (m.id && pending.has(m.id)) { pending.get(m.id).resolve(m); pending.delete(m.id); }
    82	  };
    83	  await sleep(4000); // let preload IPC + Alpine settle
    84	
    85	  // 1. preload actually loaded
    86	  chk('preload loaded (no "Unable to load preload script" in logs)', !/Unable to load preload script/.test(logBuf));
    87	  chk('no module-not-found preload crash in logs', !/module not found/.test(logBuf), logBuf.slice(-300));
    88	
    89	  // 2. bridge surface is exactly {platform, getAppVersion}
    90	  const surface = await evalJs('typeof electronAPI === "object" && electronAPI ? Object.keys(electronAPI).sort() : null');
    91	  chk('electronAPI exists', Array.isArray(surface), surface);
    92	  chk('electronAPI surface EXACTLY [getAppVersion, platform]', JSON.stringify(surface) === JSON.stringify(['getAppVersion', 'platform']), surface);
    93	  const platform = await evalJs('electronAPI.platform');
    94	  chk('electronAPI.platform is a known platform string', typeof platform === 'string' && ['darwin', 'win32', 'linux'].includes(platform), platform);
    95	
    96	  // 3. version authority round-trip
    97	  const v = await evalJs('electronAPI.getAppVersion()');
    98	  chk(`getAppVersion() === ${expectedVersion}`, v === expectedVersion, v);
    99	  chk('getAppVersion() never fabricates 1.0.0', v !== '1.0.0', v);
   100	
   101	  // 4. renderer display path (api.getStats)
   102	  const statsV = await evalJs('(function(){ try { return api.getStats().version; } catch (e) { return "THREW:" + e.message; } })()');
   103	  chk(`api.getStats().version === ${expectedVersion} (startup-resolved)`, statsV === expectedVersion, statsV);
   104	  chk('getStats().version is never the fabricated 1.0.0', statsV !== '1.0.0', statsV);
   105	
   106	  // 6. bridge minimality: no ipcRenderer/require/process reachable from the page
   107	  const leaks = await evalJs('JSON.stringify({ ipcRenderer: typeof ipcRenderer, require: typeof require, processObj: typeof process, electronAPIinvoke: typeof (electronAPI.invoke || electronAPI.send) })');
   108	  chk('page cannot reach ipcRenderer/require/process and bridge has no generic invoke/send',
   109	    leaks === JSON.stringify({ ipcRenderer: 'undefined', require: 'undefined', processObj: 'undefined', electronAPIinvoke: 'undefined' }), leaks);
   110	
   111	  const failed = results.filter((r) => !r.ok).length;
   112	  console.log(`H1-RUNTIME(${label}): ${results.length - failed} passed, ${failed} failed`);
   113	  child.kill('SIGTERM');
   114	  await sleep(800);
   115	  process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);
   116	} catch (e) {
   117	  console.error(`H1-RUNTIME(${label}) FATAL: ` + e.message);
   118	  console.error('log tail: ' + logBuf.slice(-400));
   119	  child.kill('SIGKILL');
   120	  process.exit(2);
   121	}

   211	(function resolveAppVersion() {
   212	  if (typeof electronAPI !== 'undefined' && electronAPI && typeof electronAPI.getAppVersion === 'function') {
   213	    electronAPI.getAppVersion().then(function (v) {
   214	      api._appVersion = (typeof v === 'string' && v) ? v : 'unavailable';
   215	    }).catch(function (err) {

   217	    });
   218	  } else {

    96	4. **No license granted.** `package.json` declares `"license": "UNLICENSED"` and the
    97	   repository carries no LICENSE file: all rights reserved; this public repository does
    98	   not currently grant reuse rights. Adopting a license is a product decision deferred
    99	   past 2.0.0.
   100	5. **No tracked lockfile (by policy).** The repository's verification lane is
   101	   dependency-free and `scripts/check-version-policy.js` enforces that
   102	   `package-lock.json` stays untracked. A fresh `npm install` resolves
   103	   `electron ^33.0.0` / `electron-builder ^25.0.0` by semver range; build reproducibility
   104	   is bounded by those ranges, not a pinned lock. This is an accepted, documented
   105	   trade-off of the dependency-free governance lane.
   106	6. **Vendored renderer libraries.** Three third-party libraries ship vendored under
   107	   `renderer/lib/`, outside npm governance: `alpine.min.js` (Alpine.js), `chart.min.js`
   108	   (Chart.js 4.4.7), `tailwind.js` (Tailwind CSS browser build). They load from disk
   109	   only (no CDN at runtime); no integrity hashes are recorded in-repo.
   110	7. **Electron boot smoke is structural.** `tests/e2e/flow-09` validates declaration-level
   111	   Electron structure (main/preload/webPreferences) without launching a window; no test
   112	   boots the real Electron binary in CI (CI is install-free by policy).
   113	8. **IndexedDB-specific failure paths untested in Node.** Quota-exceeded mapping and
   114	   `tx.onabort` in `renderer/js/storage-backend.js` have no automated coverage (Node
   115	   tests run on MemoryBackend); storage-failure semantics are covered at the transact
   116	   contract level (`hardening-02`).
   117	9. **No professional validation.** The product is not a professional race-engineer
   118	   replacement; outputs are advisory, in physical units, with explicit credibility and
   119	   limitations metadata (see `docs/r3-credibility-model.md`).
   120	10. **[FIXED in the v2.0.x public-release hardening line — H1; not yet in any published
   121	   release.]** ~~Preload `electronAPI` surface is not functional (pre-existing since
   122	   v1.0.0).~~ The hardening branch replaces the crashing `require('./package.json')`
   123	   with a main-process `app.getVersion()` authority over a single allowlisted IPC
   124	   channel; the renderer reports `unavailable` (never a fabricated version) if the
   125	   bridge fails. The paragraph below describes the state of the 2.0.0 tag/draft
   126	   snapshot, which predates the fix:
   127	   **Preload `electronAPI` surface is not functional (pre-existing since v1.0.0).**
   128	   `preload.js` calls `require('./package.json')`, which Electron's default-sandboxed
   129	   preload cannot resolve — the preload script fails to load and `window.electronAPI`
   130	   (`{platform, version}`) is never exposed in the packaged app. The renderer's
   131	   `typeof`-guard fallback masks this (the in-app version string falls back to
   132	   `'1.0.0'`); no other functionality depends on the preload surface. Confirmed by the
   133	   F6 packaged smoke; this is NOT an F6/2.0.0 regression (present since the initial
   134	   commit) and the fix is deferred as a production change outside the 2.0.0 release
   135	   scope. `docs/r3-data-and-privacy.md` describes the *intended* preload surface.

./docs/r3-user-workflow.md:330:**F2 ships nine end-to-end flows** under `tests/e2e/flow-{01..09}-*.test.js`, running as a Node-only logic harness (`tests/e2e/helpers/flow-harness.js`) over an in-memory `MemoryBackend()` — not a browser, DOM, Electron process, or IndexedDB, and not the first-launch surface a user would click through. Each flow exercises the specific production module surfaces its own scenario calls for — coverage is scenario-specific, not uniform: most flows drive real store/engine calls end-to-end, `flow-02` and `flow-08` mainly assert a successful golden-path landing, and `flow-09` is a declaration-level static-source check (it reads `package.json`/`main.js`/`preload.js` text and never invokes the Electron binary). Every flow ends with zero console errors. The flows cover Case creation and persistence, telemetry import and preflight, capability tier evaluation, reference-lap selection and comparison authority, Engineer Brief production, experiment planning, follow-up linking, the timeline append-only invariant (flow-06 appends an `outcome_classified` event directly rather than invoking the R3.0E classifier), and — relevant to this section — `flow-08-export-import.test.js`, the case export + reimport flow described above.

   126	   snapshot, which predates the fix:
   127	   **Preload `electronAPI` surface is not functional (pre-existing since v1.0.0).**
   128	   `preload.js` calls `require('./package.json')`, which Electron's default-sandboxed
   129	   preload cannot resolve — the preload script fails to load and `window.electronAPI`
   130	   (`{platform, version}`) is never exposed in the packaged app. The renderer's
   131	   `typeof`-guard fallback masks this (the in-app version string falls back to
   132	   `'1.0.0'`); no other functionality depends on the preload surface. Confirmed by the
   133	   F6 packaged smoke; this is NOT an F6/2.0.0 regression (present since the initial
   134	   commit) and the fix is deferred as a production change outside the 2.0.0 release
   135	   scope. `docs/r3-data-and-privacy.md` describes the *intended* preload surface.
